West has been involved in many feuds with other celebrities; *Billboard* described his feuds as either "hav\[ing] been years in the making" or "com\[ing] out of nowhere".[^cite_note-BillboardFeuds-374] [His decade-spanning feud with Taylor Swift](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Taylor_Swift%E2%80%93Kanye_West_feud), which mutually affected their reputations and cultural perception,[^cite_note-Billboard_Staff-2020-375] has been described as one of the music industry's most infamous.[^cite_note-376][^cite_note-Peters1-377] In 2018, he became embroiled in [a feud with Drake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drake%E2%80%93Kanye_West_feud) after taking an instrumental Drake wanted for the nonsense song "[Lift Yourself](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lift_Yourself)",[^cite_note-378] which journalists and fans described as trolling.[^cite_note-Pitchfork:_LiftYourself-379][^cite_note-gma-380][^cite_note-XXL_review-381] The feud has persisted despite attempts at reconciliation.[^cite_note-382] During rants in 2025, West insulted many hip-hop artists, including Drake, [Future](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Future_\(rapper\)), [Iggy Azalea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iggy_Azalea), Jay-Z, [J. Cole](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/J._Cole), [Kendrick Lamar](29909823.md), [Metro Boomin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metro_Boomin), [Playboi Carti](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Playboi_Carti), and [Travis Scott](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Travis_Scott).[^cite_note-ForbesTrolling-366][^cite_note-BillboardFeuds-374] Most did not respond to West's attacks, prompting *[Forbes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forbes)* to question whether his manner of trolling still worked,[^cite_note-ForbesTrolling-366] and his feuds with Carti and Scott had been resolved by the end of the year.[^cite_note-383][^cite_note-384]

### Social and political commentary

Main article: [Views of Kanye West](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Views_of_Kanye_West)

In a 2005 speech, West criticized both the media and the government's racial disparities in their response to [Hurricane Katrina](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hurricane_Katrina), stating on live television "[George Bush](3414021.md) doesn't care about black people".[^cite_note-385] He apologized for the comment in 2010, saying he "didn't have the grounds to call \[Bush] a racist" and later stated in regard to the remark that he "was programmed to think from a victimized mentality".[^cite_note-386] West voiced his [opposition to abortion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opposition_to_abortion) in 2013, citing his belief in the [Sixth Commandment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thou_shalt_not_kill),[^cite_note-387] and in 2022 deemed abortion "genocide and population control" of black people.[^cite_note-388] In 2018, West stated that [the 400-year enslavement of Africans](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Slavery_in_the_United_States) "sounds like a choice",[^cite_note-389] before elaborating that his comment was in reference to mental enslavement and argued for [free thought](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_thought).[^cite_note-390] He later apologized for the comment on [WQHT 107.5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/WQHT) that August.[^cite_note-391]

### Antisemitism and Nazism



Alternative cover art for one of West's albums announced in 2025



West's proposed [Sunday Service Choir](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sunday_Service_Choir) logo, based on the symbol of the *[Schutzstaffel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schutzstaffel)* (SS)

[^cite_note-392]

In late 2022, West made a series of [antisemitic](1078.md) statements,[^cite_note-cnbc-393] resulting in the termination of his collaborations, sponsorships, and partnerships with *[Vogue](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vogue_\(magazine\))*, [Universal Music Group](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Universal_Music_Group), [CAA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Creative_Artists_Agency), [Balenciaga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balenciaga), [Gap](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gap_Inc.), and [Adidas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adidas).[^cite_note-394][^cite_note-395] Several former associates of West told *Rolling Stone* that he had expressed fascination with [Adolf Hitler](2731583.md) and [Nazi Germany](21212.md) in private since the early 2000s, describing it as a "well-known but well-kept secret" within his inner circle.[^cite_note-396] In November 2022, West was widely condemned after appearing at a dinner hosted by [Donald Trump](4848272.md) at [Mar-a-Lago](1452754.md) beside [Nick Fuentes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nick_Fuentes), a [white nationalist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_nationalist).[^cite_note-cnbc-393]

In a December 2022 appearance on [Alex Jones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alex_Jones)'s *[InfoWars](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/InfoWars)*, West praised Hitler, [denied the Holocaust](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Denied_the_Holocaust),[^cite_note-397] and identified as a [Nazi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nazi).[^cite_note-timesofisraelnazi-398][^cite_note-vanityfairnazi-399] After the interview, West used his Twitter account to post a picture of a [swastika](45943.md) entangled in a [Star of David](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Star_of_David), and his account was terminated.[^cite_note-400][^cite_note-401] According to a report published by the [Anti-Defamation League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Defamation_League) (ADL) in February 2023, West's antisemitic rhetoric has caused several instances of hate-speech vandalism, harassment, and violence across the United States.[^cite_note-402]

In July 2023, [Elon Musk](909036.md) reversed the ban on his Twitter account, citing West's assurances that he would not post harmful content.[^cite_note-Busby_2023_c026-403][^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_2023_n578-404] In December, weeks before the slated release of his upcoming album, West apologized for his antisemitic remarks in a written statement on Instagram.[^cite_note-405][^cite_note-406][^cite_note-407]

In a February 2024 interview, he said "\[s]ome of the stuff I was saying was true"[^cite_note-408] and that Black people cannot be antisemitic because they "are Jew \[*[sic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sic)*]".[^cite_note-409] In February 2025, he professed his love for Hitler on Twitter and purchased local advertising during [Super Bowl LIX](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Super_Bowl_LIX) which directed viewers to his website, where he was selling a swastika T-shirt.[^cite_note-410] On May 8, coinciding with [Victory in Europe Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victory_in_Europe_Day), the 80th anniversary of the defeat of [Nazi Germany](21212.md), West released a single titled "[Heil Hitler](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heil_Hitler_\(song\))" (later renamed "Hallelujah").[^cite_note-411]

In May 2025, *Rolling Stone* wrote that West's social media tirades had turned him into "a cultural and business pariah".[^cite_note-RS:_SuingDentist-412] That month, West renounced antisemitism,[^cite_note-413] though some questioned his sincerity, with Benjamin Elterman warning that "forgiveness can be dangerous".[^cite_note-414] In November, West met with the Israeli-Moroccan [Orthodox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Orthodox_Judaism) rabbi [Yoshiyahu Yosef Pinto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yoshiyahu_Yosef_Pinto) to express remorse,[^cite_note-\:4-415] and in January 2026, published an [open letter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Open_letter) apology in *[The Wall Street Journal](173070.md)*.[^cite_note-VFOpenLetter-416] He blamed his antisemitism on his struggles with [bipolar disorder](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bipolar_disorder),[^cite_note-\:4-415] and said his outbursts stemmed from [manic episodes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Manic_episode) in which he "gravitated toward the most destructive symbol I could find, the swastika". West denied that he is a Nazi or an antisemite, and asked for "patience and understanding".[^cite_note-VFOpenLetter-416]

In April 2026, West's announcement as a headliner at [Wireless Festival](1849187.md) in London drew backlash from Jewish groups and British politicians, including Prime Minister [Keir Starmer](18681491.md), due to West's history of antisemitism.[^cite_note-417] Wireless defended the selection,[^cite_note-418] and West offered to meet with members of the UK's Jewish community.[^cite_note-419] Festival sponsors withdrew,[^cite_note-420] and the UK government blocked West from entering the country, prompting Wireless to cancel the festival.[^cite_note-421] *[The Wall Street Journal](173070.md)* stated that West's reputation "remains tainted" and would take time to recover.[^cite_note-422] Concerts in France, Italy, Poland and India were also cancelled in April and May,[^cite_note-423] but others went ahead.[^cite_note-424] A concert in Albania, arranged on the personal invitation of Prime Minister [Edi Rama](277805.md), proceeded only after a controversial €4 million government subsidy to cover unsold tickets.[^cite_note-425] Two concerts in Saint Petersburg would have made West the most prominent Western performer to play in Russia since its [invasion of Ukraine](70149799.md) before their cancellation in August.[^cite_note-426]

## Personal life

West has been one of the [wealthiest musical artists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_music_artists_by_net_worth); his net worth was as high as \$1.8 billion in 2021.[^cite_note-427] In October 2022, *[Forbes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forbes)* estimated his net worth to have dropped to \$400 million in large part due to [Adidas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adidas)'s termination of their partnership following a series of public antisemitic statements.[^cite_note-Voytko-428]

### Name change

In August 2021, West filed a petition in a California court to legally change his name from Kanye Omari West to simply Ye with [no middle or last name](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mononym);[^cite_note-\:3-429] he cited "personal reasons" for the change.[^cite_note-NameChangeUSAToday-430] The request was granted by [Michelle Williams Court](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michelle_Williams_Court), a [Los Angeles County Superior Court](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_County_Superior_Court) judge, in October 2021.[^cite_note-\:3-429] West had alluded to wishing to change his name since 2018 and had used Ye as a nickname for several years prior,[^cite_note-NameChangeUSAToday-430] stating in a 2018 interview that he believed *[ye](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ye_\(pronoun\))* ([/jiː/](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help\:IPA/English)) was the most commonly used word in the Bible:[^cite_note-432] "In the Bible it means 'you'. So, I'm you. I'm us. It's us. It went from being [Kanye](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kanye_\(name\)), which means the only one, to just Ye."[^cite_note-NameChangeIndy-433] He has said that the name Ye represents him without an ego,[^cite_note-NameChangeUSAToday-430] and has referred to "Kanye West" as his [slave name](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Slave_name).[^cite_note-434] West began releasing music under the name Ye and made an effort to change his social media handles from @kanyewest to @ye in 2025. *[HotNewHipHop](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HotNewHipHop)* noted that the public still generally referred to him using his birth name, "\[w]hether for [SEO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SEO) reasons, force of habit, animosity, or intentional suppression".[^cite_note-435] As a result, both names have remained in common use, with "Kanye West" continuing to serve as the primary identifier for much of his existing music catalogue and what he is most famously known by.

### Relationships and family

In April 2025, West said that he and a male cousin had an [incestuous](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Incest) relationship as children. The relationship began when they attempted reenacting what they saw in [gay pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gay_pornography) magazines that West had discovered in his mother's closet. West said that he performed [oral sex](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oral_sex) on his cousin until he was 14, when he ended the relationship.[^cite_note-436]

#### Kim Kardashian

In April 2012, West began dating reality television star [Kim Kardashian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kim_Kardashian), with whom he had already been long-time friends.[^cite_note-437][^cite_note-438] West and Kardashian became engaged in October 2013,[^cite_note-439][^cite_note-440] and married at [Fort di Belvedere](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Belvedere_\(fort\)) in [Florence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Florence) in May 2014, marking West's first and Kardashian's third marriage.[^cite_note-441] Their private ceremony was subject to widespread mainstream coverage, with which West took issue.[^cite_note-442] The couple's high-profile status and respective careers resulted in their relationship becoming subject to heavy media coverage; *The New York Times* referred to their marriage as "a historic blizzard of celebrity".[^cite_note-443]

West and Kardashian have four children: [North West](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/North_West_\(musician\)) (born June 2013),[^cite_note-444][^cite_note-445] Saint West (born December 2015),[^cite_note-446] Chicago West (born via [surrogate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Surrogate_pregnancy) in January 2018),[^cite_note-447][^cite_note-448] and Psalm West (born via surrogate in May 2019).[^cite_note-449][^cite_note-450] In July 2020, during a presidential campaign rally of his, West revealed that he had previously considered abortion during Kardashian's first pregnancy but has since adopted [anti-abortion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-abortion) views.[^cite_note-Henderson-451] In April 2015, West and Kardashian traveled to [Jerusalem](16043.md) to have North baptized in the [Armenian Apostolic Church](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Armenian_Apostolic_Church) at the [Cathedral of St. James](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cathedral_of_St._James,_Jerusalem).[^cite_note-452][^cite_note-453]

In September 2018, West announced that he would be permanently moving back to Chicago to establish his Yeezy company headquarters there.[^cite_note-454][^cite_note-455] This did not actually occur, and West instead went on to purchase two ranches near [Cody, Wyoming](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cody,_Wyoming), where he recorded his eighth solo studio album, *Ye*. Kardashian resides with their children in a home that the now-divorced couple owns in California,[^cite_note-Melas-456] whereas West moved into a home across the street to continue to be near their children.[^cite_note-457] In October 2021, West began the process of selling his Wyoming ranch.[^cite_note-458]

In July 2020, West acknowledged the possibility of Kardashian ending their marriage due to his adoption of anti-abortion views. Later that month, West wrote on Twitter that he had been attempting to divorce Kardashian. He also wrote that the [Kardashian family](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kardashian_family) was attempting "to lock \[him] up".[^cite_note-Henderson-451] In January 2021, [CNN](62028.md) reported that the couple were discussing divorce.[^cite_note-Melas-456] A month later, Kardashian filed for divorce,[^cite_note-459] with the couple citing "irreconcilable differences", agreeing to joint custody of their children, and declining spousal support from each other.[^cite_note-460] The divorce settlement was finalized in November 2022, and West was ordered to pay \$200,000 in monthly child support and be responsible for half of the children's medical, educational, and security expenses.[^cite_note-finalized-461]

#### Other relationships

West began an [on-and-off relationship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/On-and-off_relationship) with the designer Alexis Phifer in 2002, and they became engaged in August 2006. They ended their 18-month engagement in 2008.[^cite_note-462] Phifer stated that the pair had split amicably and remained friends.[^cite_note-463] West dated model [Amber Rose](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amber_Rose) from 2008 until mid-2010.[^cite_note-464] In an interview following their split, West stated that he had to take "30 showers" before committing to his next relationship with Kim Kardashian.[^cite_note-465] In response, Rose stated that she had been "bullied" and "[slut-shamed](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Slut-shamed)" by West throughout their relationship.[^cite_note-466]

In January 2022, actress [Julia Fox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Julia_Fox) confirmed in an *[Interview](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Interview_\(magazine\))* essay that she was dating West.[^cite_note-467] West continued to say that he wanted his "family back" and publicly lashed out at Kardashian's new boyfriend, comedian [Pete Davidson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pete_Davidson).[^cite_note-468] His treatment of Davidson and Kardashian has been described by commentators as harassment and abusive; the [64th Annual Grammy Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/64th_Annual_Grammy_Awards) dropped him as a performer in response to his "concerning online behavior".[^cite_note-469][^cite_note-470][^cite_note-471] Less than two months after confirming their relationship, Fox said that she and West had split up but remained on good terms.[^cite_note-472] She later said that she dated West purely to "give people something to talk about" during the [COVID-19 pandemic](62750956.md)[^cite_note-473] and to "get him off Kim's case".[^cite_note-474]

In January 2023, West married the Australian designer [Bianca Censori](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bianca_Censori), who works for West's Yeezy brand, in a private ceremony in Beverly Hills.[^cite_note-475][^cite_note-476] The tabloid website [TMZ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TMZ) wrote the couple did not file for a marriage license.[^cite_note-477] In response to West's subsequent trips to Australia to visit Censori's family, Australian Minister for Education [Jason Clare](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jason_Clare) commented that West may be denied a [visa](330265.md) due to his recent antisemitic remarks. The [Executive Council of Australian Jewry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Executive_Council_of_Australian_Jewry)[^cite_note-478] and the [Anti-Defamation Commission](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Defamation_Commission) further argued against granting West entry.[^cite_note-479] In April 2025, West claimed in the song "Bianca" that Censori had left him following [his controversial Twitter comments](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Views_of_Kanye_West#2025), but shared a photo later that month showing that they were still together.[^cite_note-480]

### Legal issues

See also: [List of Kanye West samples and sampling disputes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Kanye_West_samples_and_sampling_disputes)

West has been accused of the extensive use of uncleared samples in his music, leading to numerous lawsuits.[^cite_note-481] On June 29, 2022, a complaint was filed in U.S. District Court in New York claiming copyright infringement due to unauthorized use of a sample on the song "Flowers". The complaint claimed that West sampled [Marshall Jefferson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marshall_Jefferson)'s 1986 house track "[Move Your Body](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Move_Your_Body_\(Marshall_Jefferson_song\))" without gaining permission or providing compensation.[^cite_note-482]

In 2014, after an altercation with a paparazzo at the [Los Angeles Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_Airport), West was sentenced to serve two years' probation for a misdemeanor battery conviction, and was required to attend 24 anger management sessions, perform 250 hours of community service, and pay restitution to the photographer.[^cite_note-483] A separate civil lawsuit brought by the paparazzo was settled in 2015, a week before it was due for trial.[^cite_note-484] According to [TMZ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TMZ), an appeal to have West's conviction [expunged](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Expunged) from his criminal record was granted by a judge in 2016.[^cite_note-485][^cite_note-486] In July 2022, West was sued for \$416,000 by the fashion rental company the [David Casavant Archive](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_David_Casavant_Archive). The lawsuit claimed that West had failed to make payments on and return 13 rented pieces.[^cite_note-487]

West was sued three times in 2024. The first lawsuit, filed by a former security guard in April, accused him of racial discrimination, alleging he treated black employees worse than white ones.[^cite_note-488] The second, filed by his former assistant Lauren Pisciotta in June, accused him of wrongful termination, sexual harassment, and breach of contract;[^cite_note-489] Pisciotta amended the lawsuit to include sexual assault later that year, and to include stalking, sexual battery, sex trafficking, and false imprisonment in July 2025.[^cite_note-490] West denied the allegations and said he planned to countersue Pisciotta for "sexual coercion";[^cite_note-491] he settled the lawsuit in 2026.[^cite_note-492] The third, filed by a former model in November, accused him of sexual assault and strangulation. The accuser said that during a music video set, West choked and gagged her.[^cite_note-493][^cite_note-494]

West and Censori testified in a trial related to West's [Malibu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malibu,_California) mansion renovation in late February 2026. West purchased the mansion for \$57.3 million in 2021 before deconstructing it to its "bare structure" and eventually selling the property for \$21 million in 2024. The lawsuit was filed by Tony Saxon, a construction worker who was hired in 2021 to manage security and renovations at the property. He said that he was promised \$20,000 per week but was only paid once, that dangerous conditions were prevalent in the construction, and that West fired Saxon after he reported safety concerns. The trial began on February 24.[^cite_note-495] On March 11, West was found liable in a mixed verdict; the jury ordered him to pay Saxon \$140,000 in addition to attorney fees and court costs, but found he neither acted with malice nor [wrongfully terminated](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wrongful_dismissal) Saxon.[^cite_note-\:5-496]

In April 2026, an anonymous man filed a lawsuit against West, accusing him of punching and attacking him as he laid unconscious on the floor at a Los Angeles restaurant exactly two years prior. The lawsuit claimed it was a "cowardly attack" that was "shocking, physically harmful, and offensive." He further stated that West immediately returned to his security team while the anonymous man was given medical treatment following the incident. The lawsuit hints a prior interaction involving a woman in West's entourage, but was only given in a few details.[^cite_note-497]

### Religious beliefs

After the success of his song "[Jesus Walks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jesus_Walks)" from the album *[The College Dropout](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_College_Dropout)*, West was asked about his beliefs and said, "I will say that I'm spiritual. I have accepted Jesus as my Savior. And I will say that I fall short every day."[^cite_note-498] In a 2008 interview with *[The Fader](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Fader)*, West stated that "I'm like a vessel, and God has chosen me to be the voice and the connector".[^cite_note-vulturejesus-499] In a 2009 interview with online magazine [Bossip](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bossip), West stated that he believed in God, but at the time felt that he "would never go into a religion".[^cite_note-bossip-500] In 2014, West referred to himself as a [Christian](5211.md) during one of his concerts.[^cite_note-501] Kim Kardashian stated in September 2019: "He has had an amazing evolution of being [born again](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Born_again) and being saved by Christ."[^cite_note-Conner2019-502] In October 2019, West said with respect to his past, "When I was trying to serve multiple gods it drove me crazy" in reference to the "god of ego, god of money, god of pride, the god of fame",[^cite_note-Gibson2019-503] and that "I didn't even know what it meant to be saved" and that now "I love Jesus Christ. I love Christianity."[^cite_note-Schaffstall-13] During an interview with [Big Boy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Big_Boy_\(radio_host\)) in March 2024, West revealed he had "issues with Jesus" after missed prayers,[^cite_note-504] before declaring himself as God.[^cite_note-505]

### Politics

See also: [Views of Kanye West](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Views_of_Kanye_West)

In September 2012, West donated \$1,000 to [Barack Obama](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barack_Obama)'s [re-election campaign](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barack_Obama_2012_presidential_campaign); further, in August 2015, West donated \$2,700 to [Hillary Clinton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hillary_Clinton)'s [2016 campaign](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hillary_Clinton_2016_presidential_campaign). He also donated \$15,000 to the [Democratic National Committee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Democratic_National_Committee) in October 2014.[^cite_note-506][^cite_note-507] In December 2016, West met with [President-elect](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/President-elect_of_the_United_States) Donald Trump to discuss bullying, supporting teachers, modernizing curriculums, and [violence in Chicago](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gun_violence_in_Chicago).[^cite_note-508][^cite_note-509] West subsequently stated he would have voted for Trump had he voted.[^cite_note-510] In February 2017, however, West deleted all his tweets about Trump in purported dislike of the new president's policies, particularly [the travel ban](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Executive_Order_13769).[^cite_note-511] West reiterated his support for Donald Trump in April 2018.[^cite_note-512] In October 2018, West donated to [progressive](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Progressivism_in_the_United_States) Chicago mayoral candidate [Amara Enyia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amara_Enyia).[^cite_note-513]

On June 29, 2024, West traveled to [Moscow](19004.md), Russia, to celebrate the 40th birthday of the Russian fashion designer [Gosha Rubchinskiy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gosha_Rubchinskiy).[^cite_note-514] For this, the Ukrainian politician [George Tuka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Tuka) labeled West an "enemy of Ukraine"; he traveled during [the war between Russia and Ukraine](70149799.md), and Tuka said the money West spent on his trip would fund "the murder of civilians of Ukraine".[^cite_note-515]

### Mental health

West's right [frontal lobe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frontal_lobe) was damaged during his 2002 car accident, but he did not receive a formal diagnosis until 2023. According to West, the doctors' focus on his broken jaw resulted in the [brain injury](304588.md) going unnoticed, contributing to his struggles with mental illness in the following decades.[^cite_note-VFOpenLetter-416] In his song "[FML](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FML_\(song\))" (2016) and his featured verse on [Vic Mensa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vic_Mensa)'s song "[U Mad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/U_Mad)" (2015), he refers to using the antidepressant medication [Lexapro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lexapro), and in his unreleased song "[I Feel Like That](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/I_Feel_Like_That)" (2015), he mentions feeling many common symptoms of depression and anxiety.[^cite_note-516]

On November 19, 2016, West abruptly ended a concert[^cite_note-517] before being committed at the recommendation of authorities to the [UCLA Medical Center](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UCLA_Medical_Center) with hallucinations and paranoia.[^cite_note-518] While the episode was first described as one of "temporary psychosis" caused by dehydration and sleep deprivation, West's mental state was abnormal enough for his 21 cancelled concerts to be covered by his insurance policy.[^cite_note-519] He was reportedly paranoid and depressed throughout the hospitalization,[^cite_note-520] but remained formally undiagnosed.[^cite_note-521] Some have speculated that the [Paris robbery of his wife](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kim_Kardashian#Paris_robbery) may have triggered the paranoia.[^cite_note-522] On November 30, West was released from the hospital.[^cite_note-523]

In a 2018 interview, West said that he had become addicted to [opioids](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opioid) when they were prescribed to him after [liposuction](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Liposuction). The addiction may have contributed to his nervous breakdown in 2016.[^cite_note-524] In addition to his opioid addiction,[^cite_note-525] West has stated that he has had addictions to alcohol,[^cite_note-526] sex,[^cite_note-527] and pornography.[^cite_note-528][^cite_note-529] West said that he often has [suicidal ideation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicidal_ideation).[^cite_note-530] West was diagnosed with [bipolar disorder](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bipolar_disorder) in 2016,[^cite_note-531] though his diagnosis was not made public until his 2018 album *Ye.*[^cite_note-532] He told President [Donald Trump](4848272.md) that it was a [misdiagnosis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Misdiagnosis).[^cite_note-533] He had reportedly accepted the diagnosis again by 2019,[^cite_note-534][^cite_note-535] but again suggested that it had been a misdiagnosis in 2022.[^cite_note-536] However, in 2025, he attributed his then-recent antisemitic remarks to it.[^cite_note-\:4-415] In December 2022, he suggested that he may be [autistic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Autistic),[^cite_note-537] and later said in February 2025 that he had received a professional diagnosis.[^cite_note-538][^cite_note-539]

In August 2024, West's former Chief of Staff [Milo Yiannopoulos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Milo_Yiannopoulos) stated in an affidavit filed to the [California Dental Board](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/California_Department_of_Consumer_Affairs) that West was addicted to [nitrous oxide](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Recreational_use_of_nitrous_oxide).[^cite_note-540][^cite_note-541] He alleged that dentist [Thomas Connelly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thomas_Connelly_\(dentist\)), who installed titanium prosthodontics for West in January 2024, had moved into West's apartment building and was charging him \$50,000 a month to supply the drugs.[^cite_note-542][^cite_note-543] In May 2025, West and Censori sued Connelly for "medical malpractice, professional negligence, and related tortious and contractual violations". Their lawyer, Andrew Cherkasky, said that West "experienced increased confusion, mood instability, anxiety, and depression" as a result of Connelly's alleged role in facilitating the addiction.[^cite_note-RS:_SuingDentist-412]

## Musical influence

West is among the most critically acclaimed popular music artists of the 21st century, earning praise from music critics, industry peers, and cultural figures.[^cite_note-The_Perfectionism_of_Kanye_West-544][^cite_note-The_enigma_of_Kanye_West—and_how_the_world's_biggest_pop_star_ended_up_being_its_most_reviled,_too-545] In 2014, *[NME](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NME)* named him the third most influential artist in music.[^cite_note-\:1-546] *Billboard* senior editor Alex Gale declared West "absolutely one of the best, and you could make the argument for the best artist of the 21st century."[^cite_note-Ryan-253] Sharing similar sentiments, Dave Bry of *[Complex Magazine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Complex_Magazine)* called West the twenty-first century's "most important artist of any art form, of any genre."[^cite_note-complex-547] *[The Atlantic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Atlantic)* writer [David Samuels](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Samuels_\(writer\)) commented, "Kanye's power resides in his wild creativity and expressiveness, his mastery of form, and his deep and uncompromising attachment to a self-made aesthetic that he expresses through means that are entirely of the moment: rap music, [digital downloads](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Music_download), fashion, Twitter, blogs, [live streaming](18996908.md) video."[^cite_note-theatlantic-548] Joe Muggs of *[The Guardian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Guardian)* argued that "there is nobody else who can sell as many records as West does \[...] while remaining so resolutely experimental and capable of stirring things up culturally and politically."[^cite_note-549]

*Rolling Stone* credited West with transforming hip-hop's mainstream, "establishing a style of introspective yet glossy rap" while deeming him "a producer who created a signature sound and then abandoned it to his imitators, a flashy, free-spending sybarite with insightful things to say about college, culture, and economics, an egomaniac with more than enough artistic firepower to back it up."[^cite_note-RSguide-550] Writing for *[Highsnobiety](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Highsnobiety)*, Shahzaib Hussain stated that West's first three albums "cemented his role as a [progressive rap](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Progressive_rap) progenitor".[^cite_note-551] [AllMusic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AllMusic) editor Jason Birchmeier described West as "\[shattering] certain stereotypes about rappers, becoming a superstar on his own terms without adapting his appearance, his rhetoric, or his music to fit any one musical mold".[^cite_note-amg-5] Lawrence Burney of *[Noisey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Noisey)* has credited West with the commercial decline of the [gangsta rap](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gangsta_rap) genre that once dominated mainstream hip-hop.[^cite_note-Burney-552] The release of his third studio album *Graduation* has been described as a turning point in the music industry,[^cite_note-553] and is considered to have helped pave the way for new rappers who did not follow the [hardcore](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hardcore_hip-hop)-gangster mold to find wider mainstream acceptance.[^cite_note-Lamarre-554][^cite_note-555][^cite_note-Theisen,_Adam-556]

Hip-hop artists like [Drake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drake_\(musician\)),[^cite_note-557] [Nicki Minaj](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nicki_Minaj),[^cite_note-558] [Kendrick Lamar](29909823.md),[^cite_note-559] [Travis Scott](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Travis_Scott),[^cite_note-560] [Playboi Carti](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Playboi_Carti),[^cite_note-561] [Lil Uzi Vert](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lil_Uzi_Vert),[^cite_note-562] and [Chance the Rapper](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chance_the_Rapper)[^cite_note-563] have acknowledged being influenced by West. Several other artists and music groups of various genres have named West as an influence on their work.[^cite_note-583]

## Achievements

Main article: [List of awards and nominations received by Kanye West](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_awards_and_nominations_received_by_Kanye_West)

West is the fourth-[highest certified artist in the U.S.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_highest-certified_music_artists_in_the_United_States#Top_50_certified_music_artists_\(digital_singles\)) by digital singles (69 million).[^cite_note-Top_Artists_(Digital_Singles)-584] He had the most RIAA digital song certifications by a male artist in the 2000s (19),[^cite_note-RIAA_Tallies_The_Decade's_Top_Gold_&_Platinum_Award_Winners-585] and was the fourth best-selling digital songs artist of the 2000s in the U.S.[^cite_note-586] In [Spotify](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spotify)'s first ten years from 2008 to 2018, West was the sixth most streamed artist, and the fourth fastest artist to reach one billion streams.[^cite_note-587] West has the joint-most consecutive studio album to debut at number one on the *Billboard* 200 (9)[^cite_note-588] and was the first rapper to top the *Billboard* Hot 100 in [three distinct decades (2000s, 2010s, 2020s)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Billboard_Hot_100_chart_achievements_and_milestones#Most_consecutive_decades_charting_a_number-one_song).[^cite_note-589] He ranked third on *Billboard*'s 2000s decade-end list of top producers[^cite_note-590] and has topped the annual [Pazz & Jop](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pazz_&_Jop) critics' poll the joint-most times (four albums) with [Bob Dylan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bob_Dylan).[^cite_note-591]

West has been nominated for 75 [Grammys](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grammys), of which he has won 24.[^cite_note-592] He has been the most nominated act at five ceremonies,[^cite_note-593] and has received the fourth-most wins overall in the 2000s.[^cite_note-594] In 2008, West became the first solo artist to have his first three albums receive nominations for [Album of the Year](196513.md).[^cite_note-595] West has won a [Webby Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Webby_Award) for Artist of the Year,[^cite_note-596] an Accessories Council Excellence Award for being a stylemaker,[^cite_note-597] International Man of the Year at the *[GQ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GQ) Awards*,[^cite_note-598] a [Clio Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Clio_Award) for *The Life of Pablo Album* Experience,[^cite_note-599] and an honour by [The Recording Academy](8850489.md).[^cite_note-600] West is one of eight acts to have won the [Billboard Artist Achievement Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Billboard_Music_Award#Artist_Achievement_Award).[^cite_note-601] In 2015, he became the third rap act to win the [Michael Jackson Video Vanguard Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson_Video_Vanguard_Award).[^cite_note-\:2-602]

West's first six solo studio albums were included on *[Rolling Stone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rolling_Stone)*'s 2020 list of the [500 Greatest Albums of All Time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rolling_Stone's_500_Greatest_Albums_of_All_Time).[^cite_note-Rolling_Stone:_500_Greatest_Albums_of_All_Time:_2020_edition-603] *[Entertainment Weekly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Entertainment_Weekly)* named *[The College Dropout](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_College_Dropout)* the best album of the 2000s,[^cite_note-604] *[Complex](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Complex_Magazine)* named *[Graduation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Graduation_\(album\))* the best album released between 2002 and 2012,[^cite_note-605] *[808s & Heartbreak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/808s_&_Heartbreak)* was named by *Rolling Stone* as one of the 40 most groundbreaking albums of all time,[^cite_note-606] *[The A.V. Club](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_A.V._Club)* named *[My Beautiful Dark Twisted Fantasy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/My_Beautiful_Dark_Twisted_Fantasy)* the best album of the 2010s,[^cite_note-607] *[Yeezus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yeezus)* was the most critically acclaimed album of 2013 according to [Metacritic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metacritic),[^cite_note-608] and *[The Life of Pablo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Life_of_Pablo)* was the first album to top the *Billboard* 200, go platinum in the U.S., and go gold in the UK via streaming alone.[^cite_note-609][^cite_note-610]
